<script>
$(document).ready(function() {
  var headerHeight = 0;

  function dynamicHeaderHeight() {
    var $logo = $('.logo'),
        $header = $logo.closest('.header');

    $logo.on('load', function () {
      headerHeight = $header.outerHeight();
      
    }).each(function() {
      if(this.complete) {
        $(this).trigger('load');
      }
    });
  }

  var $adminBar = $('.admin-notice__bar'),
      $body = $('body.home-landing-page, body.course-landing-page, body.bundle-landing-page, body.page-template, body.coming-soon-page, body.student-certificate-page'),
      $header = $('.header'),
      $main = $('.home-landing-page main, .course-landing-page main, .bundle-landing-page main, .page-template main, .coming-soon-page main, .student-certificate-page main'),
      $dropdownToggle = $('.dropdown__toggle-button'),
      $mobileButton = $('.header__mobile-button'),
      $navigation = $('.header__nav');

  dynamicHeaderHeight();

  var adminBarHeight = $adminBar.outerHeight(),
      headerTop = $header.offset().top,
      isMobile = window.matchMedia("only screen and (max-width: 767px)");

  if(adminBarHeight != NaN) {
    $body.css({
      'margin-bottom': adminBarHeight
    });
  }

  function toggleDropdownMenu() {
    $('.dropdown').toggleClass('open');
    $dropdownToggle.attr('aria-expanded', $dropdownToggle.attr('aria-expanded') == 'true' ? 'false' : 'true');
  }
  $dropdownToggle.on('click', toggleDropdownMenu);
  $(document).on({
    keydown:function(e){
      if (e.key == "Escape" && $('.dropdown').hasClass('open')) {
        toggleDropdownMenu();
      }
    },
    click:function(e){
      if (!$(e.target).closest('.open').length && $('.dropdown').hasClass('open')) {
        toggleDropdownMenu();
      }
    }
  });
  $mobileButton.on('click', function(e) {
    $header.attr('data-menu', $header.attr('data-menu') == 'open' ? 'closed' : 'open');
    $mobileButton.attr('aria-expanded', $mobileButton.attr('aria-expanded') == 'true' ? 'false' : 'true');
    $mobileButton.toggleClass('open');
    $navigation.on('focus', false);
  });
  
    $(window).on('scroll', function (e) {
      if(!isMobile.matches) {
        if ($(window).scrollTop() > headerTop) {
          $adminBar.addClass('fixed');
          $header.addClass('fixed');
          if($header.data('menu-style') != 'floating') {
            $body.css({
              'padding-top': headerHeight,
            });
          }
        }
        if ($(window).scrollTop() <= headerTop) {
          $adminBar.removeClass('fixed');
          if($header.data('menu-style') != 'floating') {
            $body.css({
              'padding-top': 0
            });
          }
          $header.removeClass('fixed');
        }
      }
      else {
        $header.removeClass('fixed');
      }
    });
  
  $(window).on('resize', function() {
    dynamicHeaderHeight();

    

    
      if (isMobile.matches) {
        $header.removeClass('fixed');
        $body.css({
          'padding-top': 0
        });
      }
    
  });
});
</script>

  function esc(s){return (s||'').replace(/[<>&"]/g,function(m){return{'<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','&':'&amp;','"':'&quot;'}[m];});}
  function slugFrom(h){var m=(h||'').match(/\/(?:courses|bundles)\/([^\/?#]+)/);if(m)return m[1];var c=(h||'').match(/\/products\/communities\/([^\/?#]+)/);return c?c[1]:'';}
  function itemType(h){if(/\/bundles\//.test(h))return 'bundle';if(/communities\//.test(h))return 'community';return 'course';}
  var EXCLUDE_SLUGS=['partner-with-upi','learn-tarot-cards'];
  var EXCLUDE_TITLE=/UPI Study Center|Partner with UPI|center approval|Teach yourself Tarot|Corporate Partnerships|Rising Rajasthan/i;
  var CATS=['Most Popular','Gen Eds','College Prep','Business','Computer Science','Health','Math','Science','Psychology','Humanities','EFA','ACE Courses','NCCRS Courses','Free & Skill Building','English','Bundles'];
  function tagsOf(t){var o=[];CATS.forEach(function(c){if(t.indexOf(c)>=0)o.push(c);});if(t.indexOf('Computer')>=0&&o.indexOf('Computer Science')<0)o.push('Computer Science');return o;}
  function harvest(root){
    root=root||document;
    var out=[],seen={};
    
    
    var cards=root.querySelectorAll('a.card[href*="/courses/"], a.card[href*="/bundles/"], a.card[href*="/communities/"], a[href*="/courses/"], a[href*="/bundles/"], a[href*="/products/communities/"]');
    cards.forEach(function(a){
      if(a.tagName!=='A')return;
      
      if(a.closest&&(a.closest('#upi-cat')||a.closest('#upi-x')))return;
      var href=a.getAttribute('href')||'';if(!/\/(courses|bundles)\/|communities\//.test(href))return;
      var type=itemType(href);
      var slug=slugFrom(href);if(!slug||slug==='take'||seen[slug])return;
      if(/requestdocuments|request-documents/.test(slug))return;
      if(EXCLUDE_SLUGS.indexOf(slug)>=0)return; 

      
      var catEl=a.querySelector('.card__category');
      var catText=catEl?catEl.textContent.replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim():'';
      
      var nameEl=a.querySelector('.card__name');
      var title=nameEl?nameEl.textContent.replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim():'';
      
      var descEl=a.querySelector('.card__description');
      var desc=descEl?descEl.textContent.replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim():'';
      
      var imgEl=a.querySelector('img.card__img')||a.querySelector('img');
      var img=imgEl?(imgEl.getAttribute('src')||imgEl.src||''):'';
      
      var priceEl=a.querySelector('.card__price');
      var priceTxt=priceEl?priceEl.textContent.replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim():'';
      var isFree=/free/i.test(priceTxt)||/^\$?0(\.00)?$/.test(priceTxt.replace(/[^\d.$]/g,''));
      var priceNum=null;var pm=priceTxt.match(/\$\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)/);if(pm)priceNum=+pm[1].replace(/,/g,'');
      if(priceNum===0)isFree=true;
      
      var infoEl=a.querySelector('.card__product-info');
      var lessons='';if(infoEl){var lm=infoEl.textContent.match(/(\d+)\s*lessons?/i);if(lm)lessons=lm[1];}

      
      if(!title){
        var txt=(a.textContent||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();if(txt.length<6)return;
        if(!catText){var tg=tagsOf(txt);var lc='';tg.forEach(function(c){var i=txt.lastIndexOf(c);if(i>=0&&i+c.length>lc.length)lc=txt.slice(0,i+c.length);});catText=lc;}
        var after=catText?txt.slice(catText.length).replace(/^[,\s]+/,''):txt;
        var cm=after.match(/^(.*?Course)\b/);
        if(cm){title=cm[1].replace(/\s*Course$/,'').trim();if(!desc)desc=after.slice(cm[0].length).replace(/^\s*[\d.]+ average rating \(\d+ review[s]?\)\s*/,'').trim();}
        else title=after.split('  ')[0]||slug.replace(/[-_]+/g,' ');
      }
      if(!title)title=slug.replace(/[-_]+/g,' ');
      if(EXCLUDE_TITLE.test(title))return; 

      
      
      
      var tags=[];
      if(catText){
        catText.split(',').forEach(function(part){
          var c=part.replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
          if(c&&c.toLowerCase()!=='all courses')tags.push(c);
        });
      } else {
        tags=tagsOf(title);
      }
      if(type==='bundle'&&tags.indexOf('Bundles')<0)tags.push('Bundles');
      seen[slug]=1;
      out.push({slug:slug,type:type,href:(href.indexOf('http')===0?href:'https://www.upistudy.com'+href),title:title,desc:desc,img:img,tags:tags,priceTxt:priceTxt,priceNum:priceNum,isFree:isFree,lessons:lessons,cr:credits(slug),pop:tags.indexOf('Most Popular')>=0,isNew:isNewSlug(slug),kw:(NEW_KW[slug]||''),idx:out.length});
    });
    return out;
  }
  
  function mergeInto(target,batch){
    var have={};target.forEach(function(c){have[c.slug]=1;});
    batch.forEach(function(c){if(!have[c.slug]){c.idx=target.length;target.push(c);have[c.slug]=1;}});
  }
  
  function pageCount(){
    
    
    var max=1;
    document.querySelectorAll('a[href*="page="]').forEach(function(a){
      var m=(a.getAttribute('href')||'').match(/[?&]page=(\d+)/);if(m){var n=+m[1];if(n>max)max=n;}
    });
    return max;
  }
  
  
  
  
  function fetchAllPages(){
    var base=location.pathname.replace(/\/$/,'');
    var MAX_PAGES=20, CONCURRENCY=5, next=1, active=0, finished=0;
    function pumpDone(){ if(finished) return; finished=1; render(); }
    function fetchPage(pg,attempt){
      active++;
      fetch(base+'?page='+pg,{credentials:'same-origin'})
        .then(function(r){ if(!r.ok) throw new Error('HTTP '+r.status); return r.text(); })
        .then(function(html){
          var batch=harvest(new DOMParser().parseFromString(html,'text/html'));
          if(batch.length){
            if(USING_SEED){ALL.length=0;USING_SEED=false;}
            mergeInto(ALL,batch);
            render(); 
          }
          active--; pump();
        })
        .catch(function(){
          active--;
          if((attempt||0)<2){ setTimeout(function(){ fetchPage(pg,(attempt||0)+1); }, 500*((attempt||0)+1)); }
          else pump();
        });
    }
    function pump(){
      while(active<CONCURRENCY && next<=MAX_PAGES){ fetchPage(next++,0); }
      if(next>MAX_PAGES && active===0) pumpDone();
    }
    pump();
    setTimeout(pumpDone, 30000); 
  }

  var ALL=harvest();var USING_SEED=false;if(ALL.length<4){ALL=SEED;USING_SEED=true;}

  

  function hideNative(){
    
    var NATIVE_SELECTORS=[
      '.collections__container','div.collections__container',
      '.products__collections-list','ul.products__collections-list',
      '.collections__heading',
      'ul.products__list','.products__list','ul.products','section.products','.products',
      '.course-list','.courses-list','.product-list','.products-list',
      '.collection__products','.collection-products','.collection__list',
      '.product-card-list','.course-card-list','.grid__products',
      'div.products','.collections-products','.products-grid','nav.pagination'
    ];
    function isOurs(el){
      if(el.closest && (el.closest('#upi-cat')||el.closest('#upi-x')||el.closest('.upi-us-pricing')||el.closest('.upi-intl-pricing')))return true;
      if(el.querySelector && (el.querySelector('#upi-cat')||el.querySelector('#upi-x')))return true;
      return false;
    }
    NATIVE_SELECTORS.forEach(function(sel){
      document.querySelectorAll(sel).forEach(function(el){
        if(isOurs(el))return;                       
        
        if(sel==='nav.pagination'||el.querySelector('a[href*="/courses/"]')){el.style.display='none';}
      });
    });
    
    document.querySelectorAll('.upi-courses-banner').forEach(function(el){if(!el.closest('#upi-cat'))el.style.display='none';});
  }
  try{hideNative();}catch(e){}

  function isDefault(){return STATE.cat==='__all'&&!STATE.q;}
  function popularList(){return ALL.filter(function(c){return c.pop&&c.type==='course';}).slice(0,10);}

  function codeOf(title){ var m=(title||'').match(/^([A-Za-z]{2,5})\s*[- ]?\s*\d{2,3}/); return m?m[1].toLowerCase():''; }
  function norm(s){ return (s||'').toLowerCase().replace(/\./g,'').replace(/[^a-z0-9&\s]/g,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim(); }
  
  function lev(a,b){
    a=a||''; b=b||''; if(a===b)return 0; if(!a.length)return b.length; if(!b.length)return a.length;
    var prev=[],i,j; for(j=0;j<=b.length;j++)prev[j]=j;
    for(i=1;i<=a.length;i++){ var cur=[i]; for(j=1;j<=b.length;j++){ var cost=a.charCodeAt(i-1)===b.charCodeAt(j-1)?0:1; cur[j]=Math.min(prev[j]+1,cur[j-1]+1,prev[j-1]+cost); } prev=cur; }
    return prev[b.length];
  }
  
  function fuzzyHit(qtok, words){
    if(qtok.length<4)return false; 
    for(var i=0;i<words.length;i++){
      var w=words[i]; if(!w||w.length<4)continue;
      if(w===qtok)return true;
      if(qtok.length>=4 && (w.indexOf(qtok)===0 || qtok.indexOf(w)===0))return true;
      var tol = qtok.length<=6?1:2;
      if(w.charAt(0)===qtok.charAt(0) && Math.abs(w.length-qtok.length)<=tol && lev(qtok,w)<=tol)return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  
  
  function haystack(c){
    var strong=norm(c.title+' '+c.desc+' '+(c.kw||''));  
    var code=codeOf(c.title);
    if(!code && c.kw){ var cm=norm(c.kw).match(/\b([a-z]{2,5})\s?\d{2,3}/); if(cm)code=cm[1]; } 
    var tags=norm((c.tags||[]).join(' '));
    return { strong:strong, tags:tags, code:code, words:strong.split(' ').concat(code?[code]:[]) };
  }
  
  
  function wordHit(hay,q){
    if(!hay||!q)return false;
    var ws=hay.split(/[^a-z0-9&]+/);
    for(var i=0;i<ws.length;i++){ if(ws[i]&&(ws[i]===q||ws[i].indexOf(q)===0))return true; }
    return false;
  }
  
  var SYN_KEYS=null, CAT_WORDS=null;
  function expandQuery(q){
    q=norm(q);
    if(!SYN_KEYS) SYN_KEYS=Object.keys(SYN);
    if(!CAT_WORDS) CAT_WORDS=CATS.map(function(c){return norm(c);});
    var terms=[q];
    if(SYN[q]) terms=terms.concat(SYN[q]);
    var qwords=q.split(' ');
    qwords.forEach(function(w){
      if(SYN[w]) terms=terms.concat(SYN[w]);
      
      if(w.length>=4 && !SYN[w]){
        var best=null,bestD=99;
        SYN_KEYS.forEach(function(k){ if(k.indexOf(' ')<0 && k.charAt(0)===w.charAt(0)){ var d=lev(w,k); if(d<bestD && d<=2 && Math.abs(k.length-w.length)<=2){bestD=d;best=k;} } });
        CAT_WORDS.forEach(function(k){ if(k.indexOf(' ')<0 && k.charAt(0)===w.charAt(0)){ var d=lev(w,k); if(d<bestD && d<=2 && Math.abs(k.length-w.length)<=2){bestD=d;best=k;} } });
        if(best){ terms.push(best); if(SYN[best]) terms=terms.concat(SYN[best]); }
      }
    });
    var seen={},out=[]; terms.forEach(function(t){ t=norm(t); if(t&&!seen[t]){seen[t]=1;out.push(t);} });
    return out;
  }
  
  function smartMatch(c, rawQ){
    var q=norm(rawQ); if(!q)return true;
    var h=haystack(c);
    
    
    if(q.length<=3){
      if(wordHit(h.strong,q)||wordHit(h.tags,q)||q===h.code)return true;
    } else {
      if(h.strong.indexOf(q)>=0)return true;
      if(h.tags.indexOf(q)>=0)return true; 
    }
    if(h.code && (q===h.code || q.replace(/\s+/g,'')===h.code))return true;
    var qcode=q.match(/^([a-z]{2,5})\s*\d{0,3}$/); if(qcode && qcode[1]===h.code)return true;
    
    
    var terms=expandQuery(q);
    for(var i=0;i<terms.length;i++){
      var t=terms[i];
      var mt = t.length<=3 ? wordHit(h.strong,t) : (h.strong.indexOf(t)>=0); 
      if(t.length>=2 && (mt || t===h.code))return true;
    }
    
    
    var qtokens=q.split(' ').filter(function(w){return w.length>=3;});
    if(qtokens.length){
      var allHit=qtokens.every(function(tok){
        return fuzzyHit(tok,h.words) || expandQuery(tok).some(function(e){return e.length>=3 && (e.length<=3?wordHit(h.strong,e):h.strong.indexOf(e)>=0);});
      });
      if(allHit)return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  function filtered(){
    var popSlugs={};
    if(isDefault())popularList().forEach(function(c){popSlugs[c.slug]=1;});
    var list=ALL.filter(function(c){
      if(isDefault()&&popSlugs[c.slug])return false;
      if(STATE.cat!=='__all'&&c.tags.indexOf(STATE.cat)<0)return false;
      if(STATE.q&&!smartMatch(c,STATE.q))return false;
      return true;
    });
    if(STATE.sort==='az')list.sort(function(a,b){return a.title.localeCompare(b.title);});
    else if(STATE.sort==='price-lo')list.sort(function(a,b){return (a.priceNum==null?1e9:a.priceNum)-(b.priceNum==null?1e9:b.priceNum);});
    else if(STATE.sort==='price-hi')list.sort(function(a,b){return (b.priceNum==null?-1:b.priceNum)-(a.priceNum==null?-1:a.priceNum);});
    else list.sort(function(a,b){
      var ab=(a.type==='bundle'||a.type==='community')?1:0, bb=(b.type==='bundle'||b.type==='community')?1:0;
      return ab-bb || (a.isFree?1:0)-(b.isFree?1:0) || (b.pop?1:0)-(a.pop?1:0) || a.idx-b.idx;
    }); 
    return list;
  }
  function card(c){
    var META=['Most Popular','ACE Courses','NCCRS Courses','Gen Eds','College Prep','Free & Skill Building','Bundles','EFA'];
    var thumb=c.img?'<div class="thumb" style="background-image:url(\''+esc(c.img)+'\')">':'<div class="thumb ph"><span class="mono">'+esc(c.title)+'</span>';
    var cat=c.tags.filter(function(t){return META.indexOf(t)<0;})[0]||c.tags[0]||'';
    var badge=c.isFree?'<span class="free">FREE</span>':(c.isNew?'<span class="newb">&#10024; New</span>':(c.pop?'<span class="pop">&#9733; Popular</span>':''));
    var priceHtml=c.isFree
      ? '<span class="pr free">Free</span>'
      : (c.priceTxt?'<span class="pr">'+esc(c.priceTxt)+'</span>':'');
    var meta=c.lessons?'<span class="cr">'+c.lessons+' lessons</span>':'<span class="cr">ACE &amp; NCCRS</span>';
    return '<a class="c" href="'+esc(c.href)+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener">'+
      thumb+(cat?'<span class="cat">'+esc(cat)+'</span>':'')+badge+'</div>'+
      '<div class="b"><h4>'+esc(c.title)+'</h4>'+(c.desc?'<div class="dsc">'+esc(c.desc)+'</div>':'')+
      '<div class="foot">'+meta+priceHtml+'</div>'+
      '<span class="go">View course &rarr;</span></div></a>';
  }
  function renderChips(){
    var box=document.getElementById('upi-chips'),p=present();
    var known=CATS.filter(function(c){return p[c];});
    var extra=Object.keys(p).filter(function(c){return CATS.indexOf(c)<0;}).sort(); 
    var order=known.concat(extra);
    box.innerHTML='<button class="chip'+(STATE.cat==='__all'?' on':'')+'" data-cat="__all">All courses<span class="n">'+ALL.length+'</span></button>'+
      order.map(function(c){return '<button class="chip'+(STATE.cat===c?' on':'')+'" data-cat="'+esc(c)+'">'+esc(c)+'<span class="n">'+p[c]+'</span></button>';}).join('');
    
    var sel=document.getElementById('upi-catsel');
    if(sel){
      sel.innerHTML='<option value="__all"'+(STATE.cat==='__all'?' selected':'')+'>All courses ('+ALL.length+')</option>'+
        order.map(function(c){return '<option value="'+esc(c)+'"'+(STATE.cat===c?' selected':'')+'>'+esc(c)+' ('+p[c]+')</option>';}).join('');
    }
  }
  function renderPopular(){
    var wrap=document.getElementById('upi-popular-wrap');
    if(!isDefault()){wrap.style.display='none';return;}
    var pop=popularList();if(!pop.length){wrap.style.display='none';return;}
    wrap.style.display='block';
    document.getElementById('upi-popular').innerHTML=pop.map(card).join('');
    document.getElementById('upi-popct').textContent=pop.length+' picks';
  }
  function renderGrid(){
    var list=filtered();
    document.getElementById('upi-grid-title').textContent=(STATE.cat==='__all'?(isDefault()?'Explore the full catalog':'All courses'):STATE.cat);
    document.getElementById('upi-allct').textContent=list.length+' course'+(list.length!==1?'s':'');
    var grid=document.getElementById('upi-grid');
    if(!list.length){grid.innerHTML='<div class="empty">No courses match, try another search or category.</div>';document.getElementById('upi-more').style.display='none';return;}
    var vis=list.slice(0,STATE.shown),parts=vis.map(card);
    if(vis.length>=8)parts.splice(8,0,'<div class="promo"><div class="pc"><div class="pe">Best value</div><h4 class="serif">Taking several courses?</h4><p>At $250 each, 3 courses = $750. <b>PRO Lifetime is just $599</b> for all 90+ courses, forever, including future ones. (US &amp; Military)</p></div><a href="https://www.upistudy.com/enroll/2282799?price_id=4271620" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Unlock all 90+ for $599 &rarr;</a></div>');
    grid.innerHTML=parts.join('');
    var moreBtn=document.getElementById('upi-more'),remain=list.length-STATE.shown;
    if(remain>0){moreBtn.style.display='block';moreBtn.textContent='Show all courses ('+remain+' more)';}
    else moreBtn.style.display='none';
  }
  function render(){renderChips();renderPopular();renderGrid();if(typeof applyResponsive==='function')applyResponsive();}
  var io;
  function setupLazy(){
    

  }
  var calc={cred:12,region:'us'};
  function recommend(){
    var courses=Math.ceil(calc.cred/3);
    var perCost=courses*PER;
    var el=document.getElementById('upi-result');
    var name,price,why,compare='',cta,ctaHref,gold='',second='';
    if(calc.region==='intl'){
      var b;
      if(courses<=1)b={n:1,price:250,id:null};
      else if(courses<=6)b={n:6,price:1400,id:'4148333'};
      else if(courses<=12)b={n:12,price:2300,id:'4151065'};
      else b={n:24,price:4000,id:'3181059'};
      if(b.n===1){
        name='Single course';price='$250';
        why='For just <b>1 course</b>, pay per course: $250 for lifetime access and 3&ndash;4 transferable credits.';
        cta='Browse &amp; enroll &rarr;';ctaHref='#upi-grid';
      }else{
        name=b.n+'-course bundle';price='$'+b.price.toLocaleString();
        var perInBundle=Math.round(b.price/b.n);
        why='You need about <b>'+courses+' courses</b>. The <b>'+b.n+'-course bundle</b> drops your cost to ~$'+perInBundle+'/course'+(b.n===24?' and includes the UK &amp; Canada university pathway':'')+'.';
        compare='Buying '+b.n+' separately would cost <b>$'+(b.n*PER).toLocaleString()+'</b>, the bundle saves you <b>$'+((b.n*PER)-b.price).toLocaleString()+'</b>.';
        cta='Get the '+b.n+'-course bundle &rarr;';ctaHref='https://www.upistudy.com/enroll/2393590?price_id='+b.id;
      }
      second='International students aren\'t eligible for subscriptions or Lifetime, bundles are your best value.';
    }else{
      if(courses<=2){
        name=courses+' course'+(courses>1?'s':'');price='$'+perCost.toLocaleString();
        why='For <b>'+courses+' course'+(courses>1?'s':'')+'</b>, buying per course is your cheapest option: $250 each, lifetime access, no subscription needed.';
        cta='Browse &amp; enroll &rarr;';ctaHref='#upi-grid';
        second='Think you\'ll do more later? <a href="https://www.upistudy.com/enroll/2282799?price_id=4271620" target="_blank" rel="noopener">PRO Lifetime ($599)</a> pays off from your 3rd course on.';
      }else{
        name='PRO Lifetime';price='$599';gold='gold';
        why='You need about <b>'+courses+' courses</b>. Buying separately would cost <b>$'+perCost.toLocaleString()+'</b>, so <b>PRO Lifetime ($599)</b> is the smart move: all 90+ courses, forever, including future ones.';
        compare='We don\'t want you to overpay: at '+courses+' courses, Lifetime saves you <b>$'+(perCost-LIFETIME).toLocaleString()+'</b> vs. per-course, and it never expires.';
        cta='Get PRO Lifetime, $599 &rarr;';ctaHref='https://www.upistudy.com/enroll/2282799?price_id=4271620';
        second='Prefer to spread it out? The <a href="https://www.upistudy.com/enroll/2282799?price_id=4683581" target="_blank" rel="noopener">4-month plan ($279)</a> also unlocks everything while you study.';
      }
    }
    el.innerHTML='<div class="rlbl">Our recommendation</div>'+
      '<div class="rhead"><span class="rname">'+name+'</span><span class="rprice">'+price+'</span></div>'+
      '<div class="rwhy">'+why+'</div>'+
      (compare?'<div class="rcompare">'+compare+'</div>':'')+
      '<a class="ra '+gold+'" href="'+ctaHref+'"'+(ctaHref.indexOf('http')===0?' target="_blank" rel="noopener"':'')+'>'+cta+'</a>'+
      (second?'<span class="rsecond">'+second+'</span>':'');
  }
  function wireCalc(){
    var cred=document.getElementById('upi-cred');
    function upd(){calc.cred=+cred.value;document.getElementById('upi-cred-n').textContent=calc.cred;document.getElementById('upi-cred-c').textContent='\u00b7 '+Math.ceil(calc.cred/3)+' courses';recommend();}
    cred.oninput=upd;
    document.getElementById('upi-region').onclick=function(e){var b=e.target.closest('button');if(!b)return;calc.region=b.getAttribute('data-r');[].forEach.call(this.children,function(x){x.classList.toggle('on',x===b);});recommend();};
    document.getElementById('upi-help-link').onclick=function(){document.getElementById('upi-calc').scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'center'});};
    upd();
  }
  var opened={},dismissed=false;
  function tally(){if(dismissed)return;var n=Object.keys(opened).length;if(n<2)return;var spend=n*PER;if(spend<500)return;
    document.getElementById('upi-tally-msg').innerHTML='You\'ve viewed <b>'+n+' courses</b> (~$'+spend.toLocaleString()+' separately). <b>All 90+ are $599 forever</b> with PRO Lifetime.';
    document.getElementById('upi-cat-tally').classList.add('on');}
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){var a=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#upi-cat a.c');if(a){var s=slugFrom(a.getAttribute('href'));if(s){opened[s]=1;setTimeout(tally,400);}}});
  var MQ_MOBILE = (window.matchMedia ? window.matchMedia('(max-width:760px)') : null);
  function isMobileView(){
    
    
    
    var host=document.getElementById('upi-cat-orig')||document.getElementById('upi-cat');
    if(host && host.clientWidth && host.id!=='upi-cat-sticky-carrier'){ return host.clientWidth <= 760; }
    if(MQ_MOBILE) return MQ_MOBILE.matches;
    return (window.innerWidth||document.documentElement.clientWidth||9999) <= 760;
  }
  
  
  
  
  function setupDesktopSticky(){
    var bar=document.getElementById('upi-bar');
    var sentinel=document.getElementById('upi-bar-sentinel');
    var spacer=document.getElementById('upi-bar-spacer');
    if(!bar||!sentinel||!spacer) return;
    var fixed=false, relocated=false;
    var placeholder=document.createElement('div'); placeholder.style.display='none';
    bar.parentNode.insertBefore(placeholder, bar);
    
    var carrier=document.createElement('div'); carrier.className='upi-cat'; carrier.id='upi-cat-sticky-carrier';
    function hostRect(){ var h=document.getElementById('upi-cat-orig')||document.getElementById('upi-cat'); return (h&&h!==carrier)?h.getBoundingClientRect():null; }
    function applyFixedGeometry(){
      var h=hostRect(); if(!h)return;
      bar.style.left=h.left+'px';
      bar.style.right=(window.innerWidth-h.right)+'px';
      bar.style.width='auto';
    }
    function fixBar(){
      spacer.style.height=bar.offsetHeight+'px'; spacer.style.display='block';
      bar.classList.add('upi-bar-fixed');
      applyFixedGeometry();
      fixed=true;
      var t=bar.getBoundingClientRect().top;
      if(Math.abs(t)>2 && !relocated){
        
        
        var host=document.getElementById('upi-cat');
        if(host){ host.id='upi-cat-orig'; }
        carrier.id='upi-cat';
        carrier.appendChild(bar);
        document.body.appendChild(carrier);
        relocated=true;
        applyFixedGeometry();
      }
    }
    function unfixBar(){
      bar.classList.remove('upi-bar-fixed');
      bar.style.left=''; bar.style.right=''; bar.style.width='';
      if(relocated){
        placeholder.parentNode.insertBefore(bar, placeholder);
        if(carrier.parentNode)carrier.parentNode.removeChild(carrier);
        carrier.id='upi-cat-sticky-carrier';
        var orig=document.getElementById('upi-cat-orig'); if(orig){ orig.id='upi-cat'; }
        relocated=false;
      }
      spacer.style.display='none';
      fixed=false;
    }
    function onScroll(){
      if(isMobileView()){ if(fixed) unfixBar(); return; }
      var ref=fixed?spacer.getBoundingClientRect():sentinel.getBoundingClientRect();
      if(!fixed && ref.top<=0){ fixBar(); }
      else if(fixed && ref.top>0){ unfixBar(); }
      else if(fixed){ applyFixedGeometry(); }
    }
    window.addEventListener('scroll',onScroll,{passive:true});
    window.addEventListener('resize',onScroll);
    onScroll();
  }
  function applyResponsive(){
    var mobile = isMobileView();
    var sortsel=document.getElementById('upi-sort');
    var catsel=document.getElementById('upi-catsel');
    var chipsbar=document.querySelector('#upi-cat .chipsbar');
    var search=document.querySelector('#upi-cat .search');
    var selrow=document.querySelector('#upi-cat .selrow');
    
    
    if(catsel){ catsel.style.setProperty('display', mobile?'block':'none', 'important'); }
    if(chipsbar){ chipsbar.style.setProperty('display', mobile?'none':'block', 'important'); }
    if(mobile){
      if(search) search.style.flex='1 1 100%';
      if(selrow){ selrow.style.flex='1 1 100%'; selrow.style.display='flex'; selrow.style.width='100%'; }
      if(sortsel){ sortsel.style.flex='1 1 50%'; sortsel.style.width='50%'; sortsel.style.maxWidth='50%'; sortsel.style.minWidth='0'; }
      if(catsel){ catsel.style.flex='1 1 50%'; catsel.style.width='50%'; catsel.style.maxWidth='50%'; catsel.style.minWidth='0'; }
    } else {
      if(search) search.style.flex='1';
      if(selrow){ selrow.style.flex=''; selrow.style.display='flex'; selrow.style.width=''; }
      if(sortsel){ sortsel.style.flex=''; sortsel.style.width=''; sortsel.style.maxWidth=''; sortsel.style.minWidth=''; }
      if(catsel){ catsel.style.flex=''; catsel.style.width=''; catsel.style.maxWidth=''; catsel.style.minWidth=''; }
    }
  }
  function wire(){
    var q=document.getElementById('upi-q');if(q)q.oninput=function(){STATE.q=q.value.trim().toLowerCase();STATE.shown=PAGE;render();};
    var sort=document.getElementById('upi-sort');if(sort)sort.onchange=function(){STATE.sort=sort.value;renderGrid();};
    var chips=document.getElementById('upi-chips');if(chips)chips.onclick=function(e){var b=e.target.closest('.chip');if(!b)return;STATE.cat=b.getAttribute('data-cat');STATE.shown=PAGE;render();document.getElementById('upi-grid').scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'});};
    var catsel=document.getElementById('upi-catsel');if(catsel)catsel.onchange=function(){STATE.cat=catsel.value;STATE.shown=PAGE;render();document.getElementById('upi-grid').scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'});};
    var more=document.getElementById('upi-more');if(more)more.onclick=function(){STATE.shown=filtered().length;renderGrid();};
    var x=document.getElementById('upi-tally-x');if(x)x.onclick=function(){dismissed=true;document.getElementById('upi-cat-tally').classList.remove('on');};
    applyResponsive();
    window.addEventListener('resize',applyResponsive);
    window.addEventListener('orientationchange',applyResponsive);
    if(MQ_MOBILE){ try{ MQ_MOBILE.addEventListener('change',applyResponsive); }catch(e){ try{ MQ_MOBILE.addListener(applyResponsive); }catch(e2){} } }
  }
  render();wire();wireCalc();setupLazy();applyResponsive();setupDesktopSticky();
  
  try{fetchAllPages();}catch(e){}
})();
</script>
</section>

<div class="faq"><div class="wrap">
  <div class="sec-eyebrow">Questions</div>
  <h2 class="sec-title">Everything you might <em>be wondering</em>
</h2>
  <div class="faq-list">
    <details class="qa"><summary>Will my credits actually transfer to my university?</summary><div class="a">UPI Study courses carry ACE &amp; NCCRS credit recommendations, which many universities may consider for transfer credit. Transfer is always at your institution's discretion, so we recommend confirming with your advisor first — many students check using our <a href="https://www.upistudy.com/pages/upi-s-cooperating-universities">Find My College</a> tool.</div></details>
    <details class="qa"><summary>How many credits is each course worth?</summary><div class="a">Most courses carry 3 transferable credits; some lab and math courses carry 4. The credit value is shown on each course card.</div></details>
    <details class="qa"><summary>Is there a deadline to finish?</summary><div class="a">No. Every course is fully self-paced with no deadlines. You keep lifetime access to any course you purchase.</div></details>
    <details class="qa"><summary>What's the difference between buying per course and a subscription?</summary><div class="a">If you need 1–2 courses, paying $250 each is usually cheapest. If you need 3 or more (and you're a US or military student), PRO Lifetime unlocks all 90+ courses forever and saves you money. Use the calculator above to see your best option.</div></details>
    <details class="qa"><summary>Can international students enroll?</summary><div class="a">Yes — students from many countries study with us. International pricing is per-course or by multi-course bundle (subscriptions and Lifetime are for US &amp; military students).</div></details>
    <details class="qa"><summary>How do I get my official transcript?</summary><div class="a">Once you complete a course, you can request your official transcript and send your credits to your chosen university. Transcript requests are handled through your account.</div></details>
  </div>
</div></div>

<script>
(function(){
   
  function slugFrom(h){var m=(h||'').match(/\/courses\/([^\/?#]+)/);return m?m[1]:'';}
  var CRED4=['calculus-1','ace-maths-165-calculus-1','calculus2','copy-of-maths-265-calculus-ii','discrete-mathematics','biology-1','biology-2','chemistry'];
  function gather(){
    var out=[],seen={};
    document.querySelectorAll('a[href*="/courses/"]').forEach(function(a){
      var href=a.getAttribute('href')||'';var slug=slugFrom(href);if(!slug||slug==='take'||seen[slug])return;
      if(/requestdocuments|request-documents/.test(slug))return;
      var txt=(a.textContent||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();if(txt.length<6)return;
      var pop=txt.indexOf('Most Popular')>=0;
      var cm=txt.match(/([A-Z][^,]*?Course)\b/);var title=cm?cm[1].replace(/\s*Course$/,'').trim():slug.replace(/[-_]+/g,' ');
      var scope=a.closest('article,li,div')||a,img='';var ie=scope.querySelector&&scope.querySelector('img');if(ie)img=ie.getAttribute('src')||ie.src||'';
      seen[slug]=1;out.push({slug:slug,title:title,img:img,pop:pop,cr:CRED4.indexOf(slug)>=0?4:3});
    });
    return out;
  }
  var courses=gather().filter(function(c){return c.pop;});
  if(courses.length<2)courses=gather();
  if(!courses.length)courses=[{title:'PSY 101: Introduction to Psychology',img:'',cr:3},{title:'BIO 100: Introduction to Biology 1',img:'',cr:4},{title:'ECO 251: Microeconomics',img:'',cr:3}];
  var nameEl=document.getElementById('upi-ph-name'),vidEl=document.getElementById('upi-ph-vid'),crEl=document.getElementById('upi-ph-cr');
  var steps=[].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('#upi-x .scr-step'));
  var ci=0;
  function showCourse(c){
    if(nameEl){nameEl.style.opacity=0;setTimeout(function(){nameEl.textContent=c.title;nameEl.style.opacity=1;},250);}
    if(crEl)crEl.textContent=c.cr+' credits earned';
    if(vidEl){if(c.img){vidEl.classList.remove('ph2');vidEl.style.backgroundImage="url('"+c.img+"')";}else{vidEl.classList.add('ph2');vidEl.style.backgroundImage='';}}
  }
  function runSteps(){steps.forEach(function(s){s.classList.remove('act');});var i=0;(function adv(){if(i<steps.length){steps[i].classList.add('act');i++;setTimeout(adv,640);}})();}
  if(courses.length){
    showCourse(courses[0]);runSteps();
    setInterval(function(){ci=(ci+1)%courses.length;showCourse(courses[ci]);runSteps();},3500);
  }

   
  var DAYS=['Sunday','Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday'];
  var CODES=['SunSpecial10','MonSpecial10','TueSpecial10','WedSpecial10','ThuSpecial10','FriSpecial10','SatSpecial10'];
  var SEGN=8;
  var SEG_LABELS=['10% OFF','5% OFF','10% OFF','BONUS','10% OFF','5% OFF','10% OFF','BONUS'];
  var SEG_COLORS=['#216E2F','#3a7d32','#2f6e2a','#4b8a22','#216E2F','#3a7d32','#2f6e2a','#4b8a22'];
  var WIN_SEG=0; 
  var wheel=document.getElementById('upi-wheel');
  if(wheel){
    var seg=360/SEGN, stops=[];
    for(var i=0;i<SEGN;i++){stops.push(SEG_COLORS[i]+' '+(i*seg)+'deg '+((i+1)*seg)+'deg');}
    wheel.style.background='conic-gradient('+stops.join(',')+')';
    var lab='';
    for(var j=0;j<SEGN;j++){
      var mid=j*seg+seg/2;            
      lab+='<span class="lab" style="transform:rotate('+mid+'deg) translateY(-128px) rotate(90deg)">'+SEG_LABELS[j]+'</span>';
    }
    wheel.innerHTML=lab;
  }
  var spun=false,spinBtn=document.getElementById('upi-spin');
  if(spinBtn)spinBtn.onclick=function(){
    if(spun)return;spun=true;spinBtn.disabled=true;spinBtn.textContent='Spinning…';
    var seg=360/SEGN;
    var target=360*6 - (WIN_SEG*seg + seg/2); 
    wheel.style.transform='rotate('+target+'deg)';
    setTimeout(function(){
      var d=new Date().getDay();
      document.getElementById('upi-won-code').textContent=CODES[d];
      document.getElementById('upi-won').classList.add('show');
      spinBtn.textContent='🎉 You won 10% off!';
    },4800);
  };
  var copy=document.getElementById('upi-copy');
  if(copy)copy.onclick=function(){var d=new Date().getDay();if(navigator.clipboard)navigator.clipboard.writeText(CODES[d]);copy.textContent='Copied!';setTimeout(function(){copy.textContent='Copy code';},1600);};
})();
</script>
</section>
